# Spanish 02 — A2 everyday communication

Conceptual deck: `deck:es-MX:02`. Educated contemporary central Mexican Spanish;
one primary actual hidden learning point per card, production role P. Text only.
Dependencies: accepted Spanish 01 at `9285c650a53e769ec777d465bc07dd0432b7eecb`.
Guide links pin `e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f`.

## Fixed allocation and prerequisite chain

| Block / objective | IDs | Cards | Hidden objectives | Prerequisites |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| A / es-02-A | 0001–0150 | 150 | Mexican-compatible perfecto/indefinido, introductory imperfect background/routine, useful irregular pasts and event vocabulary. | Accepted 01 present/basic time frames. |
| B / es-02-B | 0151–0260 | 110 | Direct/indirect objects, reflexives, initial combinations, antecedents and recipients. | 01 introductory objects; A events. |
| C / es-02-C | 0261–0350 | 90 | Affirmative/negative commands and bounded object placement, tú/usted/ustedes instructions and requests. | A–B; 01 modals/requests. |
| D / es-02-D | 0351–0450 | 100 | Comparison/superlative and practical por/para reason, purpose, route, beneficiary. | 01 agreement/prepositions; A–C. |
| E / es-02-E | 0451–0530 | 80 | Bounded common se, progressive/periphrases, action in progress versus routine. | 01 present; B reflexives. |
| F / es-02-F | 0531–0620 | 90 | Desde/desde hace/hace, onset/elapsed duration and reference points. | A tense support; 01 time/quantity. |
| G / es-02-G | 0621–0700 | 80 | Que/quien/donde, clear antecedents and practical noun modification. | 01 reference; A–F. |
| H / es-02-H | 0701–0800 | 100 | Practical collocations/senses, repair/problem/solution/complaints, verified false-friend investigations. | A–G; primary lexical/pragmatic objective. |
| Total | 0001–0800 | 800 | Complete §6.3 plus applicable cross-cutting requirements. | Accepted 01; actual earlier dependencies per mapping. |

Allocation and system requirements derive from issue #13 and plan #2. Concrete
lexical senses and original situations are authored expansions, not official
examination inventories. New supporting words are introduced per row; visible
support is not a second taught retrieval target.

## Primary-reference decisions

- [RAE/ASALE DPD, avisar §1(a–b), second edition](https://www.rae.es/dpd/avisar): Avisar algo a alguien makes the notice a direct object and the person indirect; the entry calls this usual in American Spanish. Avisar a alguien de algo instead makes the person the direct object and introduces the notice with de. Thus 0750 keeps avisé de but uses feminine la for a mi hermana; the permitted masculine personal le does not apply. This valid de construction is not exclusive to Spain. The dar aviso expression in 0703 remains distinct.
- [RAE/ASALE Crónica 2023–2024, p.72](https://www.rae.es/sites/default/files/2024-09/Cronica%20de%20la%20lengua%20espanola%202023_2024_web.pdf#page=92): checked Mexican predominance of simple past alongside Mexico City continuative compound perfect. Temporal context governs the selected cases; no today/this-week switch or categorical perfect ban. Reference examples were not copied.
- [RAE/ASALE clitic pronouns](https://www.rae.es/dpd/pronombres%20personales%20%C3%A1tonos): checked attachment, placement as a block and tonic-personal-object doubling. Whole attached targets retain accents and whole-span support. The selected canonical object agreement does not categorically invalidate recipient-plural transfer also attested in educated Mexican usage. [Enseñar](https://www.rae.es/dpd/ense%C3%B1ar) permits the selected indirect learner reading in 0180 without invalidating its alternative direct analysis.
- [Quien](https://www.rae.es/dpd/quien), [donde](https://www.rae.es/dpd/donde), [adonde](https://www.rae.es/dpd/adonde): checked personal versus spatial antecedents, appropriate prepositions/explanatory commas, and unaccented explicit-antecedent relatives. Adonde/a donde are valid destination spellings; en donde is optional for location. Keep introductory cases within stage scope.
- DLE false-friend investigations checked the actual selected ordinary senses: [actualmente](https://dle.rae.es/actualmente) current time; [asistir](https://dle.rae.es/asistir) attending an event with a; [realizar](https://dle.rae.es/realizar) doing/carrying out; [embarazada](https://dle.rae.es/embarazado) pregnancy; [sensible](https://dle.rae.es/sensible) sensitivity; [éxito](https://dle.rae.es/%C3%A9xito) success. These context-specific decisions do not deny other dictionary senses, including assisting with asistir or the less relevant awkward sense of embarazado.
- [DEM constipado](https://dem.colmex.mx/ver/constipado), [constipación](https://dem.colmex.mx/ver/constipaci%C3%B3n), and [ASALE constipado](https://www.asale.org/damer/constipado/): investigated, without forcing a production card. DEM's broad medical retention entry and ASALE's other regional labels do not establish a single ordinary Mexican cold/constipation equation. DEM entry text was readable via the web tool despite embedded 403 notices; no unsupported frequency claim.
- [DEM componer](https://dem.colmex.mx/ver/componer) supports device repair and [foco](https://dem.colmex.mx/ver/foco) the household bulb. [CONAVI materials guide, p.44](https://siesco.conavi.gob.mx/doc/tecnicos/construccion/Recomendaciones%20para%20materiales%20de%20construccion.pdf#page=44) explicitly identifies contactos as tomacorrientes. DEM/DLE contacto alone did not settle the socket distinction and ASALE had no entry. These are vocabulary decisions, not electrical instructions or claims of exclusively Mexican usage.

[DEM corrido](https://dem.colmex.mx/ver/corrido) supports de corrido for uninterrupted activity, without implying high speed; [mano](https://dem.colmex.mx/ver/mano) supports tener a la mano for a needed item within reach. Entry text was readable despite embedded 429/403 notices.

[DEM cada](https://dem.colmex.mx/ver/cada) and [DLE día](https://dle.rae.es/d%C3%ADa) support cada tercer día for alternate-day repetition, illustrated explicitly in 0616. Día por medio was investigated: its broad American label and [ASALE country list](https://www.asale.org/damer/d%C3%ADa) did not establish the ordinary Mexican production choice for this slot; this is not a categorical ban on Mexican occurrences.

[DEM lata](https://dem.colmex.mx/ver/lata) supports dar lata for annoyance; [quedar](https://dem.colmex.mx/ver/quedar) supports quedar de/en arrangements, quedar bien social impressions and quedarse con retention. The selected complements and register do not imply exclusive Mexican usage or invalidate the other attested arrangement preposition.

Accepted 01 reference decisions remain accepted prerequisite evidence. Concrete
senses and situations outside the guide's named systems are authored expansions.

## Complete §6.3 reconciliation

Every row below records the actual hidden point, rather than crediting incidental
exposure. All 800 IDs and mappings received author/editor and complete final
model review. Stage acceptance remains ORCHESTRATOR's separate decision.

| Required family / authored expansion | Actual ID range | Reconciliation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Indefinido | 0001–0060 | Completed events, useful regular/irregular forms, meaningful accomplishment/refusal/knowledge and motion distinctions. |
| Perfecto | 0061–0100 | Auxiliary/participle and context-led continued/repeated/open experience; 0084's novelty is its favors complement, not merely recombining known forms. |
| Introductory imperfect | 0101–0135 | Childhood routine, background, prior states/capacity/permission and useful forms, distinguish completed events. |
| Event lexical expansion | 0136–0150 | Relocation, visits, changes, childhood, travel, mishaps, work and celebrations. |
| Direct/indirect objects | 0151–0200 | Person/reference roles, tonic doubling, recipient/affected owner/experiencer and infinitive attachment; 0197 missing an absent person differs from accepted 01 needing an item. |
| Reflexive verbs | 0201–0235 | Self-directed care, posture, departure/staying, movement and bounded pronominal complements. |
| Initial pronoun combinations | 0236–0260 | Recoverable item/recipient, se replacing le/les, person/number/order and compact attached forms. |
| Affirmative/negative commands | 0261–0350 | Familiar/respectful/plural address, useful regular/irregular instructions/requests and bounded object placement. No unrestricted subordinate subjunctive system. |
| Comparisons/superlatives | 0351–0400 | Degree/quantity/equality/threshold, irregular adjective/adverb forms, practical relative/absolute superlatives and change over time. |
| Por/para | 0401–0450 | Practical cause/purpose, path/destination, substitution/beneficiary, price/rate/medium, grouping/distribution, time/reference and governed/fixed collocations. Accepted 01 uses are supporting evidence, not automatically new targets. |
| Common se | 0451–0470, plus B | Bounded pronominal, reciprocal and spontaneous-change constructions; full passive/impersonal/accidental taxonomy remains 03+. |
| Present progressive | 0471–0500 | Current/temporary action, gerund formation and placement, distinct from usual routines. |
| Frequent periphrases | 0501–0530 | Recency/completion/onset/repetition/cessation/continuation/progress/imminence/pending intention/obligation/habit and practical complement distinctions. |
| Duration/reference points | 0531–0560 | Desde/desde hace/hace onset versus elapsed duration and ago, current/past reference points, negation, endpoints and duration questions. |
| Temporal lexical/functional expansion | 0561–0620 | Useful intervals, work/study/residence/waiting terms, time portions, relative reference, repetition and promptness; no swapped-number drill or claim of 90 different duration rules. |
| Que/quien/donde relatives | 0621–0660 | Recoverable subjects/objects/antecedents, bounded personal/spatial and prepositional relatives, clear modifiers without whole-clause blanks. |
| Practical noun modification | 0661–0700 | Original clothing/food/product/transport/service descriptions; distinct lexical modifiers, not extra counts for visible relative clauses. |
| Practical lexical/pragmatic expansion | 0701–0800 | Core verb complements/collocations, social commitments, repair/help, home/work, transport, shopping, health and complaints; selected verified false-friend senses in 0795–0800. |

These ranges describe reviewed families, not additional cards. New supporting
words are introduced transparently per mapping; they do not count as hidden
retrieval until actually targeted. Morphological stress, attached clitic accents,
ordinary phrase grouping and address/register extend accepted 01 foundations
without claiming recordings, native-human review or learner proficiency.

Reused verified `gpt-6.1-sol` access and Codex CLI 0.163.0 settings evidence from
accepted PR #12 in the unchanged environment; no new capability probe. Actual
processes: 20 authors, 20 separate editors and 2 final auditors, 42 distinct fresh
threads. All used high reasoning, ephemeral sequential CLI sessions, read-only
sandbox and structured JSON, with no resume, nested agents or child repository
edits. Final pass covered every card/mapping in 0001–0400 and 0401–0800; verified
findings were applied together. EXECUTOR read complete outputs, resolved findings
and alone saved source/coverage. Zero failed/retried content processes. An early
editor-prompt preparation error and a non-executable launcher invocation were
corrected before model launch; neither caused a failed or repeated content process.
Raw outputs/prompts/logs and temporary progress stay in local `/tmp`.

| Primary kind | Cards |
| --- | ---: |

| lexical | 211 |
| grammar | 409 |
| function | 8 |
| construction | 172 |

Actual lemma metadata contains 288 single-word lemma labels and 207 multiword lemma/expression labels. These are labels, not an exhaustive vocabulary total or a count of distinct dictionary senses. Per-row senses, complements, constructions and functions carry the substantive evidence; familiar visible support adds no second primary count.

There are no unresolved content findings after the complete final pass. Corrected findings and exact-revision structural/CI/preservation results are recorded in the PR. Accepted Spanish 01 and all guides are preserved; no new 01 audit, audio, export or later-stage work is included.
